## DB SCHEMA (positions)
CREATE TABLE positions (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  mint TEXT NOT NULL,
  symbol TEXT,
  qty_token REAL DEFAULT 0,
  entry_price REAL DEFAULT 0,
  entry_ts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  tp1_done INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  tp2_done INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  max_price REAL DEFAULT 0,
  stop_price REAL DEFAULT 0,
  close_ts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  close_price REAL DEFAULT 0,
  close_reason TEXT
, status TEXT DEFAULT 'OPEN');
CREATE INDEX idx_positions_mint ON positions(mint);
CREATE INDEX idx_positions_open ON positions(close_ts);
